<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu-deficient Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3中高密度堆垛层错严重抑制了宏观铁电极化，需要探索减少缺陷的方法以提升材料的多铁性能。

Method: 通过增加Cu含量制备Cu-enriched样品Cu1+xMn1-ySiTe3，利用结构表征（空间群Pm）、SHG测量、磁性和电输运测试系统研究成分-缺陷-性能关系。

Result: Cu-enriched样品几乎无堆垛层错，SHG响应显著增强；保持反铁磁序（TN~33 K）且无玻璃态，沿极性b轴出现独特的自旋翻转转变；电子基态从绝缘体转变为掺杂半导体。

Conclusion: Cu含量调控可有效消除晶体缺陷，该系统为研究成分、缺陷与多功能性质的关联提供了独特平台，为设计具有可调量子功能性的磁性极性材料开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Polar materials have recently attracted significant interest due to their rich multifunctional properties. The chalcogenide polar semiconductor Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3 (Cu-deficient) is an emerging multiferroic system in which electric polarization is coupled to magnetization. However, its macroscopic ferroelectric polarization is strongly suppressed due to the presence of a high density of stacking faults. In this work, we demonstrate that these crystal defects, likely originating from non-stoichiometry, can be substantially reduced by increasing the Cu content. Cu-enriched samples, Cu1+xMn1-ySiTe3, crystallize in a noncentrosymmetric monoclinic structure (space group Pm) as the Cu-deficient counterpart but show a nearly stacking-fault-free phase, which is attributed to the emergence of an interstitial site. Consequently, the Cu-enriched samples show a pronounced enhancement of the second-harmonic generation (SHG) response compared to Cu-deficient compositions. Magnetically, the Cu-enriched crystals retain long-range antiferromagnetic order with a Neel temperature of TN ~ 33 K without a glassy state but manifest a distinct spin-flop transition along the polar b-axis that is absent in the Cu-deficient compositions. Furthermore, the electronic ground state evolves from insulating to doped semiconducting behavior upon Cu enrichment. Together, these results establish this material system as a unique and versatile platform for elucidating the interplay among composition, crystal defects, and multifunctional properties, offering a route to design magnetic polar systems with tunable quantum functionalities.

</details>

### [93] [When Symmetries Twist: Anomaly Inflow on Monodromy Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16482)
*Christian Copetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了单值缺陷（monodromy defects）在带隙SPT相和具有反常对称性的无带隙理论中的性质，发现背景磁通作为反常源，导致单值缺陷只能定义为对称性生成元与反常诱导拓扑序之间的畴壁，并带来受保护的手性边缘模和绝热泵浦等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单值缺陷（拓扑对称性算符的动态终止）在存在反常对称性时的定义和性质，特别是背景磁通作为反常源如何影响缺陷的拓扑结构。

Method: 采用inflow机制分析，结合SPT相和反常对称性的理论框架，在连续介质和格点模型中验证对反常手征对称性单值缺陷的预测。

Result: 单值缺陷只能定义为对称性生成元与反常诱导拓扑序之间的畴壁；缺陷世界体积上存在受保护的手性边缘模；局域磁通束缚的无带隙自由度出现绝热泵浦现象。

Conclusion: 单值缺陷的拓扑修饰（topological dressing）导致了一系列可观测的物理后果，包括手性边缘模和绝热泵浦，这些预测在反常手征对称性的连续和格点模型中得到验证。

Abstract: Monodromy defects describe a dynamical termination of topological symmetry operators, and are sourced by a localized background magnetic flux. We study their properties in gapped SPT phases and, by inflow, in gapless theories with an anomalous symmetry. The background flux acts as a source for the anomaly, impacting the definition of the monodromy defect, which can only be defined as a domain wall between the symmetry generator and a topological order induced by the anomaly. The topological dressing has several consequences, such as the presence of protected chiral edge modes on the defect's worldvolume, and the adiabatic pumping of gapless degrees of freedom bound to the localized flux. We verify our predictions in several examples, focusing on monodromy defects for anomalous chiral symmetries, both in the continuum and on the lattice.

</details>


### [94] [Isolated Critical Points for Scherk-Schwarz Compactifications of M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16484)
*Zihni Kaan Baykara,Shi Chen,Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了M理论在具有非平凡自旋结构的环面紧致化（Scherk-Schwarz紧致化）中的孤立临界点，通过识别未破缺对偶群及其在模空间上的不动点，在d=8,6,5,4维找到了孤立临界点。同时提出了M理论在T^n被离散Z_N对称性轨道折化下的对偶协变反常抵消条件，推广了微扰弦轨道折的水平匹配条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找M理论Scherk-Schwarz紧致化势能中的孤立临界点，并建立与弦论轨道折类比的反常抵消条件。

Method: 利用未破缺对偶群在模空间上的不动点来定位孤立临界点，提出了对偶协变的反常抵消条件。

Result: 在d=8,6,5,4维找到孤立临界点实例，并给出广义反常抵消条件。

Conclusion: Scherk-Schwarz紧致化存在孤立临界点，且反常条件推广了弦论的水平匹配条件。

Abstract: We consider Scherk-Schwarz compactifications of M-theory (toroidal compactifications with a non-trivial spin structure) in various dimensions and find isolated critical points of the potential on the moduli space. We demonstrate this by identifying the unbroken duality group and finding isolated points on the moduli spaces which are fixed by elements of the unbroken duality group. We work out concrete examples involving compactifications down to $d=8,6, 5$ and $4$ spacetime dimensions. We also conjecture a duality covariant anomaly cancellation condition for M-theory on $T^n$ orbifolded by discrete $\mathbb Z_N$ symmetries acting as phases on the charge lattice. This anomaly cancellation condition generalizes the level matching requirement for perturbative string orbifolds.

</details>


### [95] [5d Trinions and Tetraons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16497)
*Mario De Marco,Michele Del Zotto,Michele Graffeo,Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在M理论几何工程框架下，构造了具有D型味对称性的三叉和四叉5d SCFT，对应非线性广义quiver，展示了新的瞬时子对称性增强模式，并排除了E型三叉和四叉结构的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展5d SCFT的原子分类方案，从线性广义quiver推广到非线性结构，探索更丰富的5d SCFT构造可能性。

Method: 采用M理论几何工程方法，构造三叉(trinion)和四叉(tetraon)5d SCFT，分析其味对称性为D型，并研究其融合产生的奇异几何。

Result: 成功构造了D型三叉和四叉5d SCFT，发现其对应非线性广义quiver并展现新的瞬时子对称性增强模式；同时排除了E型三叉和四叉的存在。

Conclusion: 5d SCFT的原子分类可推广至非线性结构，但受限于D型对称性，E型三叉和四叉结构不存在。

Abstract: Recently, an atomic classification scheme of 5d SCFTs has been proposed, relying on the identification of indecomposable building blocks that can be fused together to produce large classes of 5d SCFTs. These novel SCFTs are known as bifundamental 5d conformal matter theories, and their fusion produces linear generalized quivers. We generalize such picture employing M-theory geometric engineering to construct trinions and tetraon 5d SCFTs with flavor symmetry of type D. They correspond to non-linear generalized quivers displaying novel patterns of instantonic symmetry enhancement, and their fusion produces singular geometries that often are non-toric non-complete intersections. Finally, within our setup, we rule out trinions and tetraons of type E.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate Krylov complexity in a simple quantum mechanical model describing a black hole coupled to its radiation. The model is constructed as a simplified ``mini-BMN" matrix system inspired by a recent proposal of Maldacena. Our aim is not to reproduce the full dynamics of the BMN matrix model, but rather to isolate a tractable setting in which the information-theoretic behaviour of a radiating black hole can be studied explicitly. We analyze both the early- and late-time behaviour of Krylov complexity and the associated Krylov entropy. At early times, perturbative and numerical analyses reveal the expected growth characteristic of chaotic quantum dynamics. At late times, however, the dynamics saturates to a plateau, consistent with equilibration between the black hole and its radiation and with general expectations from finite-entropy quantum systems. We argue that this plateau behaviour admits a semiclassical interpretation in terms of Euclidean instanton contributions in an effective path-integral. The toy model studied here offers a controlled framework in which these features can be investigated analytically and numerically.

Method: 利用超对称局域化将可观测量约化为矩阵模型，在大$N$极限下解析计算自由能和$\frac{1}{2}$-BPS圆形威尔逊圈，并通过重新求和处理非平面修正。

Result: 对于任意$N_f$，大't Hooft耦合$\lambda$下的非平面修正无穷级数可被有效耦合$\widetilde\lambda$重新求和。在非共形窗口($0\leq N_f<4$)，首次通过局域化得到强耦合平面极限显式结果；在超共形情形($N_f=4$)，建议$\widetilde\lambda$而非$\lambda$作为AdS/CFT中的体弦耦合。

Abstract: We study a class of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ SU($N$) gauge theories with two massless hypermultiplets in the rank-two antisymmetric representation and $0\leq N_f\leq 4$ fundamental flavors. These theories are superconformal for $N_f=4$ and asymptotically free otherwise. Supersymmetric localization on the four-sphere applies in both cases and leads to matrix-model representations for a broad class of protected observables. Within this framework, we compute the free energy and the expectation value of the $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS circular Wilson loop in the large-$N$ limit. For any $N_f$, we show that the infinite series of non-planar corrections at large 't Hooft coupling $λ$ can be resummed in terms of an effective coupling $\widetildeλ$. In the non-conformal window ($0\leq N_f<4$), this provides a first example in which localization leads to explicit results in the planar limit at strong 't Hooft coupling. In the superconformal case ($N_f=4$), the large-$N$ expansion in $\widetildeλ$ suggests a refined AdS/CFT dictionary in which $\widetildeλ$, rather than $λ$, plays the role of the bulk string coupling.

</details>

Method: 在3d玩具模型中引入保持广义共形结构的质量项，在2-loop水平计算2点和3点关联函数，研究质量标度以下的IR结构，论证超越微扰论的IR有限性。

Conclusion: 3d玩具模型在引入适当质量项后表现出超越微扰论的IR有限性，全息对偶地对应4d宇宙学中UV有限性（无宇宙学奇点），该方法可推广到更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper we consider IR divergences in a 3d toy model field theory for 4d holographic cosmology, and we analyze them by introducing a mass term in a way that preserves a certain form of the generalized conformal structure. This allows us to compute 2- and 3-point functions at 2-loops and study their IR structure below the mass scale, from which we argue for a possible IR finiteness beyond perturbation theory, consistent with lattice results. In the holographically dual 4d cosmology, this corresponds to UV finiteness, i.e., the absence of cosmological singularities. The 3d IR field theory methods could be extended beyond this specific application.

### [99] [Interpolating conformal algebra in $(1+1)$ dimensions between the instant form and the light-front form of relativistic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19251)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过构建(1+1)维插值共形代数及其4×4投影时空矩阵表示，系统分析各插值形式下共形代数六个生成元中运动学与动力学生成元数量的变化规律。

Result: 在(1+1)维共形代数中，LFD的运动学生成元数量最大化(4个)、动力学生成元数量最小化(2个)；IFD则相反(运动学2个、动力学4个)；插值形式介于两者之间。此外给出了(1+0)和(0+1)维共形群的泡利矩阵表示及量子简谐振子表示。

Conclusion: LFD在(1+1)维量子场论中具有显著优势，能最大程度地将动力学问题转化为运动学问题，从而节省大量动力学计算工作。插值共形代数为理解不同动力学形式之间的连续过渡提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present the interpolating conformal algebra between the instant form dynamics (IFD) and the light-front dynamics (LFD) in $(1+1)$ dimensions, along with a $4\times4$ interpolating projective spacetime matrix representation. While there are six generators in the $(1+1)$ dimensional conformal algebra, the number of kinematic and dynamic generators dramatically changes in LFD, maximizing (minimizing) the number of kinematic (dynamic) generators to four (two) with respect to two (four) kinematic (dynamic) generators in IFD, as well as in any other forms of dynamics between IFD and LFD. It confirms and signifies the utility of LFD, saving substantial dynamical efforts in solving the $(1+1)$ dimensional quantum field theories. We also present $2\times2$ Pauli matrix representation of $(1+0)$ and $(0+1)$ conformal groups, and creation/annihilation operators of quantum simple harmonic oscillator representations of $(1+0)$ dimensional conformal groups.

</details>


### [100] [Evaporating Black Hole Interior and Complexity Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16619)
*Nicolò Bragagnolo,S. Prem Kumar*

TL;DR: 本文在Jackiw-Teitelboim引力中通过末端世界膜模型研究蒸发黑洞内部演化，发现子系统复杂度在Page时间前线性增长、达到峰值（~O(S_BH)）后指数衰减，且后期相对涨落变大导致自平均性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究蒸发黑洞内部复杂度演化的非微扰引力效应，揭示蒸发过程与不蒸发黑洞复杂度行为的本质区别。

Method: 利用JT引力+EoW膜模型模拟黑洞蒸发，通过边界到膜两点函数的测地线长度作为子系统复杂度度量，采用淬火无序平均（含时空虫洞和副本虫洞的量子引力效应）进行计算。

Result: 蒸发黑洞复杂度演化：早期线性增长→Page时间达峰值O(S_BH)→指数衰减；复杂度相对涨落Page时间前很小，之后变得O(1)并持续增大，丧失自平均性。

Conclusion: 黑洞蒸发过程导致复杂度演化与不蒸发情形定性不同，且依赖于辐射希尔伯特空间维度；后期复杂度被稀有位形主导而非典型实现。

Abstract: We study the evolution of the interior of an evaporating black hole in a simple model of Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity with an end-of-the-world (EoW) brane, where evaporation is modeled by entangling the brane's internal states with an auxiliary radiation system. To probe the black hole interior, we consider a geodesic length extracted from a boundary-to-brane two-point function and interpret its renormalised value as a measure of subsystem complexity. Our computation, based on quenched disorder averaging, includes non-perturbative gravitational effects from both spacetime wormholes and replica wormholes, encoding ensemble averaging over the dual random Hamiltonian and brane-state couplings. Unlike non-evaporating black holes where complexity first grows linearly and then plateaus at late times $\sim{\cal O}(e^{S_{\rm BH}})$, we find that complexity evolution of the black hole subsystem in the evaporating case differs drastically, depending nontrivially on the dimension of the emitted radiation Hilbert space. It grows linearly at early times, reaches a maximum at Page time $\sim{\cal O}({S_{\rm BH}})$, and then decays exponentially. We further show that the relative fluctuations of the interior length remain small before the Page time but become of order one and eventually large at later times: this signals a loss of self-averaging, with the ensemble-averaged complexity dominated by rare configurations rather than by typical realisations.

</details>


### [101] [Examples of Invertible Gauging via Orbifold Data, Zesting, and Equivariantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16942)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和统一三维拓扑场论中可逆对称性规范化的不同数学方法，为对称性分类和缺陷结构提供系统框架。

Method: 采用等变化、G-交叉辫状zesting和广义轨形构造三种方法，通过具体案例（Dijkgraaf-Witten Z₂理论、Tambara-Yamagami范畴、Chern-Simons SU(2)ₖ理论）进行说明和比较，并引入zested轨形数据概念建立等价性。

Result: 阐明了三种方法之间的内在联系；发现了对称性通过zesting关联的两个轨形数据是Morita等价的，即共享相同的表面缺陷。

Conclusion: 等变化、G-交叉辫状zesting和广义轨形构造是处理三维可逆对称性规范化的等价框架；zested轨形数据的Morita等价性揭示了对称性规范理论间的深层结构关系。

Abstract: We study the gauging of invertible symmetries, particularly in 3 dimensions, using equivariantisation, $G$-crossed braided zesting, and the generalised orbifold construction. We discuss how these methods are related and illustrate them in various examples. We cover all $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetries in Dijkgraaf--Witten $\mathbb{Z}_2$-gauge theory $\mathcal{D}(\mathbb{Z}_2)$, the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetries described by Tambara--Yamagami categories, and obstructions to gauging the central symmetry in Chern--Simons $\mathrm{SU}(2)_k$-gauge theory. We introduce zested orbifold data for symmetries related by zesting and show that the two associated orbifold data are Morita-equivalent, i.e.\ they have the same underlying surface defect.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the reconstruction of a free quantum field in 4-dimensional Lorentzian Anti-de-Sitter (AdS$_4$) spacetime in terms of primary operators in the boundary 3d CFT (CFT$_3$). We show that the positive and negative energy subspaces of solutions to the Klein-Gordon equation in AdS can be spanned with bulk-to-boundary propagators with appropriate time orderings. As a result, free scalar fields on a codimension-1 bulk hypersurface $Σ_τ$ can be expressed in terms of operators integrated over boundary regions in the past or future of $Σ_τ$ with kernels given by time-ordered or anti-time-ordered propagators. We present various equivalent representations for the bulk field in terms of either CFT primaries or their shadows. We show from both a representation theoretic perspective and by direct computation of various flat space limits that our construction is the AdS analog of the canonical quantization of a free scalar in flat space. Depending on the choice of $Δ$ and the location of the boundary insertions one obtains a decomposition of the scalar in either a plane wave basis ($Δ\rightarrow \infty$) or a Carrollian basis (fixed $Δ$). Finally, we show that the free scalar in AdS$_4$ can be alternatively decomposed in terms of wavefunctions associated with principal series representations of dimension $δ= 1 + iλ$ of an $\mathfrak{so}(3,1)$ subalgebra of the AdS$_4$ isometry algebra. We demonstrate that the latter become, in the limit of large AdS radius and for fixed $δ$, scalar conformal primary wavefunctions in flat space.

</details>

Method: 采用可分离主方程方法处理五维Myers-Perry黑洞的电磁和引力扰动；在静态极限下将方程简化为Heun方程，并利用其特殊类得到超几何函数表示的解析解；通过主场重构规范场；使用修正球谐函数展开分析响应模式；迭代计算静态潮汐Love张量。

Abstract: We study the separable master equations for the electromagnetic and gravitational perturbations in five-dimensional Myers-Perry black holes. In the static limit, while the master equation for the electric polarization of the Maxwell field reduces to that of a massless scalar field, the magnetic polarization and gravitational perturbation yield Heun equations for both its angular and radial components. Remarkably, these Heun equations fall into a special class that admits exact analytic solutions in terms of hypergeometric functions. We reconstruct the gauge field using master fields and study its asymptotic behavior. When expanding the result in the basis of modified spherical harmonics, we find modes with higher angular momentum arise in response to the excitation of sources with lower angular momentum. The static tidal Love tensor that characterizes such mixing structure of the response can be computed iteratively. We also discuss the possible near zone approximation of the master equations for the magnetic polarization.

### [104] [Nonlinear response of the chiral magnetic effect in the D3/D7 holographic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16931)
*Masataka Matsumoto,Mirmani Mirjalali,Ali Vahedi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用D3/D7全息对偶系统，通过旋转探针D7膜引入轴化学势实现手征磁效应，研究在手征磁效应相与绝缘相相边界附近，手征磁流对外磁场的非线性响应，发现电流-磁场呈现多值依赖关系，并证明轴化学势与外磁场协同稳定系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索手征磁流在外磁场作用下的非线性响应特性，揭示相边界附近的新奇物理行为，理解轴化学势与磁场的协同作用对系统相结构和稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用D3/D7膜全息对偶框架，通过旋转探针D7膜引入轴化学势实现手征磁效应，重点分析绝缘相与手征磁效应相边界附近的非线性电流-磁场关系，并研究绝缘相的动力学稳定性。

Result: 在相边界附近，手征磁流对外磁场呈现多值依赖关系，表现出高度非线性特征；轴化学势与外磁场共同作用能够稳定系统。

Conclusion: 揭示了轴化学势与磁场对系统相结构和稳定性的协同影响，发现了全息规范理论中手征输运的新非线性特性。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear response of the chiral magnetic current to an external magnetic field in a holographic setup. Using the D3/D7 brane system, where the chiral magnetic effect (CME) can be realized by considering rotating probe D7-branes, corresponding to introducing an axial chemical potential, we analyze the current-magnetic field relation beyond the linear regime. Focusing on the vicinity of the phase boundary between the insulating phase and the CME phase, we find that the chiral magnetic current exhibits a multi-valued dependence on the magnetic field, indicating a highly nonlinear response characteristic of this model. We further study the dynamical stability of the insulating phase near the transition point, and show that the presence of both an axial chemical potential and an external magnetic field cooperatively stabilize the system. Our results clarify the interplay between the axial chemical potential and the magnetic field in determining the phase structure and stability of the system, and reveal new nonlinear aspects of chiral transport in holographic gauge theories.

</details>


### [105] [Semi-classical Imprint of Horizon Induced Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17586)
*Arnab Chakraborty,Onirban Islam,Arshad Momen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究圆上平方可积函数空间中倒谐振子的态密度，利用驻相近似计算时间演化算符的振荡积分表示，证明热化是经典Lyapunov不稳定性的半经典体现，并通过谱分析填补了先前文献中的概念和数学空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献通过启发式解析延拓方法报告了倒谐振子与热化现象的联系，但存在概念和数学上的不严谨。本文旨在通过严格的谱分析来填补这些空白，并阐明热化作为经典Lyapunov不稳定性的半经典体现。

Method: 在L²(S)空间上考虑倒谐振子，利用时间演化算符Schwartz核的振荡积分表示，采用驻相近似计算态密度。

Result: 成功计算了倒谐振子的态密度，验证了热化现象确实是经典Lyapunov不稳定性的半经典表现，并指出了先前文献中的概念和数学缺陷，通过谱分析予以修正。

Conclusion: 倒谐振子在圆上的量子行为可解释热化现象，其为经典混沌（Lyapunov不稳定性）的半经典体现，且通过严格的振荡积分与谱分析，本文弥补了此前研究的不足。

Abstract: We consider an inverted harmonic oscillator in the space $L^{2} (\mathbb{S})$ of square-integrable functions on the circle $\mathbb{S}$ and compute its density of states employing the stationary phase approximation. Our computation is based on an oscillatory integral representation of the Schwartz kernel of the time-evolution operator. This demonstrates thermalisation as a semi-classical manifestation of the classical Lyapunov instability -- reported earlier in [Phys. Rev. D 102, 044006; Phys. Rev. D 102, 124047] using heuristic analytic continuation. Our spectral analysis of the Hamiltonian points out and closes the conceptual and mathematical gaps in the preceding literature.

</details>


### [106] [Superform Approach to Equivariant Localization in Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17919)
*Michele Galli,Christian Kennedy,Parth Raina,Gabriele Tartaglino-Mazzucchelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文识别了超引力中等变局域化背后的超空间机制，证明闭超形式在超对称背景上生成等变闭多形式，并在4维N=2共形超引力中为矢量、线性多重态及手征和BF作用量原理构造了这些多形式，为超引力中BPS可观测量的等变局域化提供了几何基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超引力中的等变局域化建立几何基础，特别是将超空间形式体系与等变上同调联系起来，以推广到更高阶导数理论和全息对偶。

Method: 通过超空间形式体系，证明闭超形式在超对称背景上可生成等变闭多形式，并具体在4维N=2共形超引力中构造了矢量、线性多重态以及手征和BF作用量原理对应的多形式。

Result: 成功构造了多种超多重态对应的等变闭多形式，复现并扩展了近期相关结果，为超引力中BPS可观测量（包括高阶导数理论）的等变局域化提供了第一步几何框架。

Conclusion: 该超空间机制为超引力中的等变局域化和全息对偶研究提供了新的几何工具，具有推广到更多超对称背景和理论的潜力。

Abstract: We identify a superspace mechanism behind equivariant localization in supergravity. We show that closed superforms generate, on supersymmetric backgrounds, equivariantly closed polyforms. After presenting the general mechanism, we construct such polyforms for vector and linear multiplets, and for chiral and BF action principles, in off-shell $4d$ $\mathcal{N}=2$ conformal supergravity, reproducing and extending recent results. Our construction provides a geometric first step toward equivariant localization of BPS observables in supergravity, including higher-derivative theories, and holography.

</details>


### [107] [Five legs @ three loops: N=4 sYM amplitude near mass-shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17056)
*Andrei V. Belitsky,Leonid V. Bork,Roman N. Lee,Vladimir A. Smirnov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在平面最大超对称杨-米尔斯理论中，对五个近零质量W玻色子的散射振幅进行了三圈分析。通过六维N=(1,1)超杨-米尔斯理论的幺正切割技术建立主积分基，降维到四维后生成内外质量，进而下降到特殊Coulomb分支。所有积分在姊妹论文中已明确计算，最终得到该红外敏感观测量的简洁表示，并确认了红外项和有限项的指数化。红外双对数展现出通过八边形反常维度控制的普遍性，且发现三个独立运动学结构各自具有不同的't Hooft耦合函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先前两圈结果扩展到三圈，以验证红外发散的指数化结构以及八边形反常维度作为控制系数的普遍性，同时探究不同运动学结构是否具有独立的耦合函数行为。

Method: 采用六维N=(1,1)超杨-米尔斯理论中的幺正切割技术建立主积分基，通过降维到四维产生内外质量，然后下降到特殊Coulomb分支将所有传播子质量设为零。利用姊妹论文中计算的积分显式表达式进行分析。

Result: 得到了该红外敏感观测量的简洁表示，确认了红外项和有限项的指数化行为。红外双对数的控制系数为八边形反常维度。与两圈结果不同，三个独立运动学结构各自拥有独立的't Hooft耦合函数。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了红外发散的指数化普遍性，并揭示了在高阶微扰论中不同运动学结构需要独立的耦合函数来描述，这是对之前两圈认识的深化。

Abstract: We present a three-loop analysis of the scattering amplitude of five nearly massless W-bosons in planar maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory. The basis of the master integrals is established, making use of the unitarity-cut sewing technique in six-dimensional N=(1,1) super-Yang-Mills theory. Its dimensional reduction down to four allows us to generate masses for internal and external states. We descend on the special Coulomb branch of maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory by setting all propagator masses to zero. Employing explicit expressions for all integrals that we calculated in a companion paper, we find a concise representation for this infrared-sensitive observable. We confirm its exponentiation, both for infrared and finite terms. The infrared double logarithm manifests the anticipated universality through the octagon anomalous dimension as its governing coefficient. Unlike our previous two-loop result, this consideration reveals that each of the three independent kinematic structures furnishing the amplitude possesses its own function of 't Hooft coupling.

</details>


### [108] [The classical Yangian symmetry of Auxiliary Field Sigma Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18213)
*Daniele Bielli,Christian Ferko,Michele Galli,Gabriele Tartaglino-Mazzucchelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，用于理解可积场论中经形变（如TT变形或辅助场变形）后的守恒荷及其Yangian代数结构。通过推广BIZZ递归程序，给出了荷满足Yangian代数的充分条件，并在多种可积sigma模型及其形变中验证了Yangian对称性和Maillet括号结构的普适存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积场论经形变（如TT变形或辅助场变形）后，其无限多对称性的结构变得模糊不清，现有理论缺乏对这些形变后守恒荷及其代数结构的系统理解。

Method: 推广了BIZZ（Brezin, Itzykson, Zinn-Justin, Zuber）递归程序来生成非局域荷，并给出了这些荷满足Yangian代数的充分条件；将该方法应用于多种可积sigma模型及其辅助场形变。

Result: 在所有考察的可积sigma模型及其辅助场形变中，均发现了Yangian代数和Maillet括号结构，证明了哈密顿可积性和Yangian对称性在形变后的持久性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解形变后可积场论的对称性结构提供了统一的理论框架，解释了为何在广泛的形变sigma模型中哈密顿可积性和Yangian对称性仍得以保持。

Abstract: Integrable field theories exhibit infinitely many symmetries which underlie their solvability, but the structure of these symmetries can become obscured after performing an integrable deformation such as $\TT$ or an auxiliary field deformation. In this paper, we present a systematic organizing principle for understanding deformed charges and their Yangian structure in a broad class of integrable sigma models and their auxiliary field deformations. We generalize the recursive procedure of Brezin, Itzykson, Zinn-Justin, and Zuber (BIZZ) for generating non-local charges, and give sufficient conditions under which the resulting charges obey a Yangian algebra. We apply these results to many examples of integrable sigma models and their auxiliary field deformations, finding a Yangian algebra and Maillet bracket structure in all cases. This offers a unified explanation for the persistence of Hamiltonian integrability and Yangian symmetry across a wide landscape of deformed sigma models.

</details>


### [109] [Five legs @ three loops: slightly off-shell dual conformal integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17057)
*Andrei V. Belitsky,Leonid V. Bork,Roman N. Lee,Andrei I. Onishchenko,Vladimir A. Smirnov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了N=4 SYM理论特殊Coulomb分支上三圈五点振幅的三圈主积分。对于真正的五边形积分，采用保持双共形不变性(DCI)的正则化方法。引入新方法从每个区域贡献中分解出DCI交叉比依赖，剩余积分通过取外部不变量极限简化。对于最复杂积分I_5^{(3)}的82个区域中的3个，采用参数表示中的IBP归约并用HyperInt评估局部有限积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算N=4 SYM理论中三圈五点振幅的主积分，特别是处理具有复杂结构的三圈五边形积分。

Method: 采用保持DCI的正则化方法处理五边形积分；引入新方法分解DCI交叉比依赖；通过取外部不变量极限简化积分；对困难区域使用参数表示中的IBP归约和HyperInt评估。

Result: 成功处理了最复杂积分I_5^{(3)}的82个区域中的79个，其余3个区域通过IBP归约和HyperInt完成评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为N=4 SYM理论中三圈五点振幅的完整计算奠定了基础，提供了处理复杂多圈多腿积分的新方法。

Abstract: We calculate the three-loop master integrals contributing to the three-loop five-point amplitude on the special Coulomb branch of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theory. For the genuine pentagon integrals, we follow the approach of Ref. [JHEP 12 (2025) 107], which includes a regularization preserving dual conformal invariance (DCI). As a new ingredient, we introduce a simple method, allowing to factor out the dependence on the DCI cross ratios from the contribution of each region. The remaining integrals are then essentially simplified by taking successive limits of vanishing external invariants. For 3 out of 82 regions contributing to the most complicated integral $\mathcal{I}_5^{(3)}$ we were not able to perform the integration even after these simplifications. For these three regions, we perform the integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction in parametric representation and evaluate the resulting locally finite integrals using HyperInt.

</details>


### [110] [From classical Lax ODEs to quantum integrable theories: the moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18695)
*Davide Fioravanti,Marco Rossi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文建立了经典可积系统（广义sinh-Gordon方程）与量子可积模型之间的对应关系，通过线性矩阵微分问题的Wronskian连接系数构造出Baxter Q/T算子的特征值，并推导出Y-系统和热力学Bethe ansatz方程，最终证明了Zamolodchikov的两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典可积偏微分方程的相容性条件出发，建立与量子可积模型之间的对应关系（ODE/IM对应），并引入模参数（moduli）对已知关系进行推广和修正。

Method: 以依赖2N-1个复系数的广义sinh-Gordon方程为出发点，通过其矩阵线性微分问题不同解之间的Wronskian连接系数，构造Baxter Q和T算子的特征值，并由此推导Y-系统和TBA方程，整个过程无需经由散射理论。

Result: 得到了包含模参数修正的泛函关系、Y-系统和TBA方程，在零动量极限下完整描述了量子齐次sine-Gordon模型（对应N=4 SYM强耦合下的散射振幅或AdS_3中的对偶Wilson环），并证明了Zamolodchikov基于先前结果的两个猜想。

Conclusion: 本文通过ODE/IM对应框架，将经典可积系统与量子可积模型联系起来，引入模参数的依赖关系是关键创新，无需散射理论即可直接得到TBA方程，为单值性空间提供了不变参数化，并验证了已有的理论猜想。

Abstract: The general idea of this paper is to start from a classical integrable (partial differential) equation which arises as a compatibility condition for a matrix linear differential problem. For definitiveness' sake, a generalised sinh-Gordon equation depending on $2N-1$ complex coefficients or moduli is considered. Then, the connexion coefficients (Wronskians) of different solutions to this problem satisfy, in the spirit of the Ordinary Differential Equation/Integrable Model correspondence, functional relations, which can be considered, -- if supplemented by suitable asymptotic behaviours --, as identifying a specific state of a quantum integrable model: in fact they are the eigenvalues of extensions of Baxter operators $Q$ and $T$, the transfer matrix. Moreover, Y-system and (implementing the asymptotic conditions) thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz equations originate from them, without any passage through the scattering theory, and providing an invariant parametrisation of the monodromy space. The crucial novelty is the modification of all the relations because of their dependence on the moduli. For zero momentum, they fully describe physically the quantum homogeneous sine-Gordon model, {\it i.e.} scattering amplitudes of gauge fields in $\mathcal{N} = 4$ SYM at strong coupling or their dual null polygonal light-like Wilson loops in $AdS_3$. As a direct consequence of the correspondence, two Zamolodchikov's conjectures, based on previous results, are also proven.

</details>


### [111] [Covariant extrinsic curvature expansion of the nonlocal effective action for a massless scalar field on a manifold with boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17124)
*A. Boasso,C. D. Fosco,B. C. Guntsche,F. D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用热核方法推导了弯曲边界上无质量标量场的非局域有效作用量在外曲率张量的二次阶协变展开，该框架统一了Monge-patch嵌入的早期结果并将其推广至更一般的曲面，并应用于2+1维振荡变形环和3+1维振荡变形球中的粒子产生率计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于弯曲边界上标量场非局域有效作用量的研究局限于Monge-patch嵌入描述，无法处理一般曲面。本文旨在建立更几何化的框架，克服这一限制，并应用于粒子产生等物理问题。

Method: 采用热核方法（heat-kernel approach），在平坦流形上带弯曲边界的系统中，对非局域贡献进行外曲率张量的二次阶协变展开。

Result: 推导出了外曲率张量二次阶的非局域有效作用量协变展开式，该展开在外曲率梯度占主导而非线性曲率效应显著的区域内有效。作为应用，计算了2+1维振荡变形环和3+1维振荡变形球的粒子产生率。

Conclusion: 本文建立的几何框架不仅统一了Monge-patch方法的结果，还将其推广到更一般的曲面，为处理弯曲边界系统中的非局域效应提供了更普适的工具，并通过粒子产生率的计算展示了其实用性。

Abstract: We study the nonlocal effective action of a massless scalar field defined on a flat manifold with a curved boundary. Using a heat-kernel approach, we derive a covariant expansion of the nonlocal contribution to quadratic order in the extrinsic curvature tensor. Our construction provides a geometric framework that both reproduces earlier results obtained for Monge-patch embeddings and extends them to more general surfaces that need not admit a global Monge-patch description. The expansion is valid in the regime where gradients of the extrinsic curvature dominate over nonlinear curvature effects. As an application, we compute the particle-creation rate for an oscillating deformed ring in $2+1$ dimensions and an oscillating deformed sphere in $3+1$ dimensions.

</details>


### [112] [Emergent AdS Geometry and Black Hole Thermodynamics from Functional Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17209)
*Ki-Seok Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在功能重正化群框架下，为d维欧氏空间中的O(N)矢量模型构建了一个非微扰全息对偶描述，将Wilson重正化群迭代生成的尺度坐标识别为(d+1)维 emergent 体时空的径向方向，建立了双向全息词典，并在无质量临界构型下自发组织出无坐标奇点的稳定规则AdS_{d+1}几何。通过消除热视界附近的锥形缺陷奇点，严格证明了半经典霍金温度与边界场论温度相等，并证明近视界热力学势精确满足黑洞热力学第一定律，从而从第一原理自下而上推导出Bekenstein-Hawking面积律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为O(N)矢量模型建立非微扰的全息对偶描述，从第一性原理出发理解全息原理与AdS/CFT对应的微观起源，克服传统微扰方法的局限，通过FRG框架将重正化群流与全息体时空几何建立直接联系。

Method: 利用功能重正化群(FRG)框架，通过连续迭代Wilsonian RG变换，将尺度坐标识别为 emergent 体时空的径向方向；构造双向全息词典，将非微扰涨落映射到 emergent 体度量翘曲因子；通过消除热视界附近的锥形缺陷奇点来匹配温度，并验证近视界热力学势满足第一定律。

Result: 在无质量临界构型下，emergent 引力真空自发组织为稳定的、规则的AdS_{d+1}几何（无坐标奇点），满足所有局域能量条件；半经典霍金温度精确等于边界场论温度；近视界热力学势满足黑洞热力学第一定律，自下而上推导出Bekenstein-Hawking面积律S_{horizon}=N/4·A。

Conclusion: 本文成功在FRG框架内为非微扰O(N)矢量模型构建了自洽的全息对偶，从第一原理导出了AdS几何和黑洞热力学性质，为理解全息原理的微观机制提供了新的非微扰途径。

Abstract: We present a non-perturbative holographic dual description for the \(O(N)\) vector model in \(d\)-dimensional Euclidean space within the functional renormalization group (FRG) framework. By continuously iterating Wilsonian RG transformations, the extra-dimensional scale coordinate is identified as the radial direction of an emergent \((d+1)\)-dimensional bulk spacetime. We construct a bidirectional holographic dictionary that maps non-perturbative fluctuations directly into the emergent bulk metric warping factors. Under the massless critical configuration, the emergent gravitational vacuum spontaneously organizes into a stable, regular Anti-de Sitter (\(\text{AdS}_{d+1}\)) geometry without coordinate singularities, satisfying all foundational local energy conditions. Near the thermal horizon, by systematically eliminating the conical deficit singularity, we rigorously prove that the semiclassical Hawking temperature identically matches the boundary field theory temperature (\(T_H \equiv T\)). Finally, we show that the near-horizon thermodynamic potentials exactly satisfy the First Law of Black Hole Thermodynamics, spontaneously generating the Bekenstein-Hawking area law (\(S_{\text{horizon}} = \frac{N}{4}\mathcal{A}\)) from a first-principles, bottom-up derivation.

</details>


### [113] [Functional Renormalization Group as a Ricci Flow: An $\mathcal{F}$-Entropy Perspective on Information Metric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17215)
*Ki-Seok Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了功能重正化群(FRG)与由微分同胚修正的Ricci流之间的等价关系。通过将Polchinski精确重正化群方程重构成无穷维Fokker-Planck框架，证明耦合常数空间上Fisher信息度量的演化是一个几何优化过程。关键构造是场论$\mathcal{F}$-熵泛函——Perelman $\mathcal{F}$-熵泛函的无穷维类比——作为理论的Lyapunov势。证明了场分布的连续标度演化构成该$\mathcal{F}$-熵的黎曼梯度流，进而通过熵景观的参数Hessian变形耦合常数空间上的信息度量。有效作用量的对数作为标量势$\Phi$生成确保流张量一致性的微分同胚。该框架表明，高能自由度的逐次积分有效平滑了信息流形的曲率，驱动系统趋向Ricci孤子平衡。这些结果为刻画RG不动点的稳定性提供了新基础，并在量子场论、信息几何与Perelman几何演化理论之间建立了第一性原理桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立功能重正化群(FRG)与Ricci流之间的严格数学等价关系，揭示重正化群流的内在几何结构，为量子场论提供新的几何化视角，并建立量子场论、信息几何与Perelman几何演化理论之间的桥梁。

Method: 将Polchinski精确重正化群方程重构为无穷维Fokker-Planck框架；构造场论$\mathcal{F}$-熵泛函作为Perelman熵的无穷维类比；利用Fisher信息度量作为耦合常数空间上的信息度量；通过有效作用量的对数作为标量势$\Phi$生成微分同胚以保证张量一致性；证明场分布演化构成$\mathcal{F}$-熵的黎曼梯度流。

Result: 建立了FRG与由微分同胚修正的Ricci流之间的等价性；证明了场分布的连续标度演化是$\mathcal{F}$-熵的黎曼梯度流；证明了高能自由度逐次积分平滑信息流形曲率，驱动系统趋向Ricci孤子平衡；揭示了有效作用量的对数作为生成微分同胚的标量势$\Phi$的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为刻画RG不动点的稳定性提供了新基础，为量子场论提供了信息几何与Ricci流视角的新理解，在量子场论、信息几何与Perelman几何演化理论之间建立了第一性原理层面的联系。

Abstract: We establish an equivalence between the Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) and the Ricci flow modified by a diffeomorphism. By reformulating the Polchinski exact renormalization group equation into an infinite-dimensional Fokker-Planck framework, we show that the evolution of the Fisher information metric on the coupling constant space is a geometric optimization process. Central to this mapping is our construction of a field-theoretic $\mathcal{F}$-entropy functional - an infinite-dimensional analogue of Perelman's $\mathcal{F}$-entropy functional - which acts as a Lyapunov potential for the theory. We prove that the continuous scale evolution of the field distribution constitutes a Riemannian gradient flow of this $\mathcal{F}$-entropy, which in turn deforms the information metric on the coupling constant space via the parametric Hessian of the entropic landscape. Crucially, the log of the effective action serves as a scalar potential $Φ$ that generates the diffeomorphisms required to ensure the tensorial consistency of the flow. Our framework demonstrates that successive integration of high-energy degrees of freedom effectively smooths out the curvature of the information manifold, driving the system toward a Ricci soliton equilibrium. These results provide a novel foundation for characterizing the stability of RG fixed points and offer a first-principles bridge connecting quantum field theory, information geometry, and Perelman's theory of geometric evolution.

</details>


### [114] [Field Theory Models for a Holographic Superconductor in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18628)
*Salvatore Santoro,Roberto Auzzi,Stefano Bolognesi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文通过Robin边界条件研究全息超导体的场论模型，利用大c因子化、模不变性和金兹堡-朗道理论，分析了二维CFT中相关双迹扰动下的相图、凝聚体临界行为以及分数磁通涡旋现象，并将其解释为分数Little-Parks效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全息超导体中场论模型的相变机制，特别是由Robin边界条件诱导的凝聚行为，并建立全息模型与边界场论之间的对应关系。

Method: 采用大c因子化假设分析受相关双迹扰动形变的二维CFT相图；利用模不变性联系高低温相，解析再现全息模型中零绕数扇区的结果；使用有效金兹堡-朗道场论描述匹配凝聚体的近临界行为；通过场论玩具模型研究分数磁通涡旋并解释为分数Little-Parks效应。

Result: 成功解析再现了全息超导体零绕数扇区的相图结果；凝聚体的近临界行为与金兹堡-朗道描述匹配；发现了携带分数磁通量子涡旋的场论玩具模型。

Conclusion: Robin边界条件可以诱导全息超导体的凝聚，边界CFT的双迹扰动形变与全息模型等价，且该体系支持分数Little-Parks效应。

Abstract: We investigate field theory models of holographic superconductors in which the condensation of the order parameter is induced by a Robin boundary condition. Assuming large-$c$ factorization, we study the phase diagram of a two-dimensional CFT deformed by a relevant double-trace perturbation. Using modular invariance, we relate the high- and low-temperature phases, reproducing analytically the results for the zero-winding sector of the holographic model. Moreover, we match the near-critical behaviour of the condensate with an effective Ginzburg--Landau field theory description. Another important feature of the holographic superconductor is the presence of vortices that carry fractional magnetic flux. We investigate a field theory toy model with similar properties and interpret it as a fractional Little--Parks effect.

</details>


### [115] [Anomaly-Free Spectra, Unimodular Lattices and 6D R-Symmetry Gauged Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17643)
*Katrin Becker,Qi You*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有规范阿贝尔R对称性和一个张量多重态的6D $\mathcal N=(1,0)$超引力的分类问题，提出了11个满足局域Green-Schwarz因子化条件及全局一致性条件的新模型，表明无反常规范$U(1)_R$超引力景观比以往认识更丰富但仍高度受限，并分析了反常系数的算术结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无反常6D超引力中规范$U(1)_R$对称性的分类空间，填补现有枚举文献在低秩模型上的空白，澄清无反常谱、全局一致性约束与弦论/F-理论UV实现之间的边界。

Method: 基于局域Green-Schwarz因子化条件及多个全局一致性条件（包括秩二单模电荷格嵌入性、特征向量条件、无鬼规范场条件），通过算术分析反常系数结构，系统搜索满足$G_{\text{非阿贝尔}}\times U(1)_R$规范群的11个新模型。

Result: 发现11个新模型，低秩模型恰好是现有文献最不直接适用的部分；证明了$n_V \equiv 8 \pmod{12}$是秩二情况下单模可嵌入性的充要条件；验证了特征向量条件在实现的支路上的充分性，并识别了一个仍需额外排除的剩余支路；给出了$D_4$李代数对反常多项式的详细贡献。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了规范$U(1)_R$超引力景观比先前认识更丰富但仍受高度约束，为区分可UV实现的理论与仅满足局部反常抵消的理论提供了更精确的判据。

Abstract: We study the classification problem for anomaly-free 6D $\mathcal N=(1,0)$ supergravities with a gauged abelian R-symmetry and one tensor multiplet. We present eleven new models with gauge group $G_{\mathrm{non-Abelian}}\times U(1)_R$ that satisfy the local Green--Schwarz factorization condition, together with several recently proposed global consistency conditions. In particular, the low-rank models we found are precisely where some of the recent enumeration literature is least directly applicable. These examples suggest that the landscape of anomaly-free gauged $U(1)_R$ supergravities may be richer than previously recognized while still remaining highly constrained. We analyze the arithmetic structure of the anomaly coefficients, including their integral pairings, embeddability into rank-two unimodular charge lattices, the characteristic-vector condition and ghost-free gauge-field conditions. We show that $n_V \equiv 8 \pmod{12}$ is necessary and sufficient for the unimodular embeddability in the rank-two case. For the characteristic-vector condition we verify sufficiency for the branches realized by our examples and identify a remaining branch requiring additional exclusion. We also present a detailed discussion of the contribution to the anomaly polynomial when the $D_4$ Lie algebra is present. These results sharpen the boundary between anomaly-free 6D spectra, global-consistency constraints, and possible UV realization in string theory or F-theory.

</details>


### [116] [An Alternative Viewpoint on Kinematic Flow from Tubing Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17751)
*Ji-Yuan Ke,Ping He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过对共形耦合标量场在幂律宇宙学中的波函数系数所满足的微分方程进行重新表述，提出了一种基于图管（graph tubings）的动力学流新视角。通过反转管道的演化方向构建分裂规则，推导出微分方程，并揭示了奇异性、局域演化等物理结构。在时间排序基下，该规则暗示了时间如何从动力学空间涌现，并可推广至tr φ³理论，表明管道及动力学流可能比微分方程更为基本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视并理解宇宙学波函数系数所满足的微分方程背后的深层物理结构，探索动力学流（kinematic flow）与图管（graph tubings）能否作为比微分方程更基本的数学对象，并揭示时间如何从动力学空间中涌现。

Method: 将波函数系数的微分方程重新表述为基函数的迭代微分系统；利用图管对基函数进行编码；通过反转管道演化方向重新审视动力学流关系；构建等价于树级动力学流的分裂规则来推导微分方程；引入基于时间排序的替代基展开分析。

Result: 建立了基于反转图管演化方向的分裂规则，成功推导出树级微分方程；揭示了微分方程中蕴含的奇异性与局域演化结构；在时间排序基下发现时间从动力学空间涌现的机制；将该结论从单个费曼图推广至tr φ³理论。

Conclusion: 图管与动力学流可能是比微分方程更为基本的数学对象，它们具有独立的物理意义；时间可以从动力学空间中通过特定规则涌现，这一图景不限于特定费曼图，具有更广泛的普适性。

Abstract: The differential equations satisfied by the wavefunction coefficients of conformally coupled scalars in a power-law cosmology can be recast into an iterative differential system of basis functions. These functions can be encoded within graph tubings, and are governed by a set of rules describing how they flow in kinematic space. In this paper we propose a new viewpoint on the kinematic flow by reformulating the relations among these basis functions through reversing the evolution direction of the tubings. The differential equations can then be derived by constructing appropriate splitting rules equivalent to the kinematic flow (at tree level). While the implementation of these rules can be somewhat complicated, they reveal richer physical structures underlying the differential equations, such as singularities and local evolution. Under an alternative basis based on time ordering, these rules offer important implications for how time emerges from kinematic space. This conclusion is even not restricted to individual Feynman diagrams, and can be generalized to the tr $φ^3$ theory. This suggests that the tubings, as well as the kinematic flow, might be more fundamental objects than the differential equations, and have a life of their own.

</details>


### [117] [A Renormalizable and Unitary Approach to Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17817)
*D. G. C. McKeon,F. T. Brandt,J. Frenkel,S. Martins-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入拉格朗日乘子场，将爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用的量子修正限制在单圈阶，从而构建一个可重整化且幺正的模型，并在经典极限下重现爱因斯坦场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦-希尔伯特引力在量子化后出现不可重整化且违反幺正性的问题，亟需一个既保持可重整化又满足幺正性，同时能正确回归经典引力的理论框架。

Method: 通过引入一个拉格朗日乘子场，对量子修正施加约束，使其仅保留至单圈阶，从而在保持幺正性的前提下实现模型的可重整化。

Result: 该模型在单圈阶同时满足可重整化和幺正性，并且经典极限下能够还原标准爱因斯坦场方程。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日乘子场方法为量子引力理论的构建提供了一条兼顾理论自洽性与经典对应性的可行路径。

Abstract: A Lagrange multiplier field restricts the quantum corrections to the Einstein-Hilbert action at one-loop order, yielding a model that is renormalizable and unitary while reproducing the Einstein field equations in the classical limit.

</details>


### [118] [Properties of the quantum vacuum in non-abelian gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18220)
*Fernando Ezquerro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰蒙特卡洛模拟分析了非阿贝尔规范理论的量子真空性质，重点研究了边界效应对Casimir能量的贡献、不同边界条件的影响，以及最轻胶球质量是否主导Casimir能量的指数衰减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清非阿贝尔规范理论中Casimir能量在大边界间距L下的行为机制，特别是从微扰（无质量）到非微扰（有质量）过渡区的特殊行为，并验证最轻胶球质量是否负责Casimir能量的指数衰减。

Method: 使用非微扰蒙特卡洛模拟方法计算量子真空能量，分析不同边界条件对真空能量的影响，并关联胶球质量与Casimir能量的衰减特性。

Result: 规范理论在紫外区（无质量）和红外区（有质量）之间表现出特殊的Casimir能量行为转变；低能谱中存在非零质量的胶球态，最轻胶球质量可能主导Casimir能量的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 纯规范理论中Casimir能量的指数衰减可由最轻胶球质量解释，为理解非阿贝尔规范理论的真空结构提供了非微扰视角。

Abstract: In this thesis we analyze the quantum vacuum properties of non-abelian gauge theories. We calculate the energy of the quantum vacuum by non-perturbative methods using Monte Carlo simulations, focusing on the contribution of boundary effects to the Casimir energy. In particular, we analyze the dependence of the vacuum energy on the types of boundary conditions. The main goal is to clarify the behaviour of this energy for large separation L between the boundaries of the domain where the fields are confined. Usually this Casimir energy decreases polynomially with L for massless theories and exponentially for massive theories. Since gauge theories interpolate between these two regimes, being massless in the ultraviolet regime and massive in the infrared regime, one expects a very special change of behaviour from the perturbative to the non-perturbative approaches. In pure gauge theories there is evidence of the existence of glueball states in the low energy spectrum with a non-vanishing mass, the second goal will be testing if the mass of the lightest glueball is responsible for the exponential decay of the Casimir energy of gauge theories.

</details>


### [119] [Renormalisation Group Invariants from Scaling and Non-overlapping Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18341)
*Apostolos Pilaftsis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了尺度对称性与非重叠全局对称性的协同作用如何导致多标量场势参数中的重整化群不变量（RGIs），并通过标度不变场方向证明了双线性场算符RGIs在全圈阶的识别与构造，最后讨论了该形式体系对规范层级问题的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找多标量场理论中参数之间的重整化群不变量关系，以简化理论分析并探讨规范层级问题的可能解决方案。

Method: 提出一种"spurion-field"（spurion场）构造方法，利用尺度对称性与非重叠全局对称性的协同作用，并借助标度不变场方向来识别和构造双线性场算符在所有圈阶的RGIs。

Result: 成功构建了多标量场模型中参数间的重整化群不变量，在简单非超对称模型和双希格斯二重态模型等近期文献场景中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该spurion-field形式体系为处理规范层级问题提供了新的视角和工具，有望在更广泛的量子场论框架中应用。

Abstract: We show how the synergy of scaling and non-overlapping global symmetries can lead to Renormalisation Group Invariants (RGIs) among the parameters of potentials with multiple scalars. The instrumental role of scale-invariant field directions in the identification and construction of RGIs for bilinear field operators to all loops is demonstrated. We present a few illustrative examples to showcase our constructive spurion-field approach, which is applied to simple non-supersymmetric models as well as to scenarios reported recently in the literature that include the two-Higgs doublet model. The implications of our spurion-field formalism to address the gauge-hierarchy problem are discussed.

</details>


### [120] [Tachyonic (In)stability in Randall-Sundrum Braneworld Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18403)
*Abhirup Karmakar,Soumitra SenGupta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究膜世界场景中辐射场的不稳定性与自发标量化的可能性，分析了Randall-Sundrum两膜模型在三种情况下的快子不稳定性条件，并扩展到f(R)引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Damour-Esposito-Farése(DEF)模型中自发标量化的启发，探究膜世界场景中是否同样存在快子不稳定性与自发标量化的现象。

Method: 采用Randall-Sundrum两膜模型，将额外维度的辐射场作为标量场，分别在三种情景下分析辐射场的快子（不）稳定性：1) 含精细调谐条件、辐射势为零的RS模型；2) 无精细调谐、辐射势仅来自引力部分的RS模型；3) 含体稳定化场的RS模型。进一步在Planck膜上引入FLRW背景几何，分析稳定性条件。最后将形式推广到f(R)引力理论。

Result: 含体稳定化场时，膜上T>0的物质会改变辐射场的真空期望值(VEV)，破坏规范层级问题的解决；而T<0的物质不影响辐射场的稳定性和VEV。导出了Planck膜上FLRW背景中辐射场快子（不）稳定性的精确条件。

Conclusion: 膜世界场景中辐射场存在快子不稳定性与自发标量化的可能，且物质能量-动量张量的迹对辐射场稳定性和VEV有重要影响；该形式可推广到f(R)引力理论。

Abstract: Low-energy effective theories provide a natural description of four-dimensional physics in higher-dimensional geometries, where the imprint of the bulk geometry appears as parameters of the lower-dimensional theory. Inspired by the Damour-Esposito-Farése (DEF) model of spontaneous scalarization in first generation Scalar-Tensor theories of gravity, we investigate the possibility of tachyonic instability and spontaneous scalarization in braneworld scenarios. We consider the two-brane Randall-Sundrum model where the low-energy effective theory on either brane is of scalar-tensor nature with the extra-dimensional radion playing the role of the scalar. We have determined the possibilities for tachyonic (in)stability of the radion field on either branes in three scenarios: the Randall-Sundrum (RS) model with fine-tuning conditions in which the potential of the radion field vanishes identically, the RS model without fine-tunings where the radion potential arises purely from the gravity sector, and the RS model with a bulk stabilizing field that generates a radion potential with a minimum. With the bulk stabilizing field, we have found that on-brane matter with $T>0$ changes the VEV of the radion, destroying the resolution of the gauge hierarchy problem, whereas on-brane matter with $T<0$ does not alter the stability and VEV of the radion. We further determined the exact condition of tachyonic (in)stability of radion field on Planck brane with the FLRW background geometry on the brane. Finally, we have extended the same formalism to study the tachyonic (in)stability conditions of $f(R)$ theories of gravity with the motivation that the 4D effective theory on RS branes can be casted into a $f(R)$ theory on the branes at the inflationary epoch of cosmology.

</details>


### [121] [Monodromy of Calabi-Yau threefold flops via grade restriction rule and their quantum Kahler moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18514)
*Ban Lin,Mauricio Romo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于等级限制规则和窗口分类，给出了与特定Calabi-Yau三维流形flops相关的单值性的精确表达式，并展示了基于半球配分函数的B-膜电荷格点上的单值性作用的一般公式，通过量子Kähler模空间中判别式的显式形式进一步精化了单值性的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算与Calabi-Yau三维流形flops相关的单值性，并建立其与嵌套环面链基本群的联系，以深化对量子Kähler模空间结构的理解。

Method: 利用等级限制规则和窗口分类方法，结合阿贝尔和非阿贝尔规范线性sigma模型的半球配分函数，推导B-膜电荷格点上的单值性作用公式；通过量子Kähler模空间中判别式的显式形式进一步精化单值性。

Result: 得到了与Calabi-Yau三维流形flops相关的单值性的精确表达式，以及B-膜电荷格点上单值性作用的一般公式，并在多个例子中通过判别式与嵌套环面链基本群的关系进一步精化了单值性形式。

Conclusion: 本文的方法为理解Calabi-Yau三维流形上的单值性结构提供了精确的代数工具，并揭示了其与拓扑链结构的深层联系。

Abstract: We present exact expressions, based on the grade restriction rule and window categories, for monodromies associated to certain Calabi-Yau threefold flops. We show a general formula for the monodromy action on the lattice of B-brane charges, based on the hemisphere partition function for abelian and nonabelian gauged linear sigma models. We exploit the explicit form of the discriminant in the quantum Kähler moduli to further refine the form of the monodromies, in several examples, using their relation to the fundamental group of nested torus links.

</details>


### [122] [The Large Vector Multiplet and Gauging $(2,2)$ $σ$-models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18536)
*Dmitri Bykov,Ulf Lindström,Martin Roček*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，最近提出的一个新规范多重态是大矢量多重态（LVM）的有约束或部分对偶化版本。使用该多重态进行规范耦合会产生一个与sigma模型相互作用的(2,2) βγ系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清新提出的规范多重态与已有的LVM之间的关系，理解其物理本质。

Method: 通过约束或部分对偶化的方式，将LVM转化为新规范多重态，并分析其规范耦合后的结果。

Result: 新规范多重态是LVM的有约束或部分对偶化版本；使用它进行规范耦合会得到(2,2) βγ系统与sigma模型的相互作用。

Conclusion: 新提出的规范多重态并非独立的新结构，而是LVM的一种变形，其规范耦合会产生特定的相互作用系统。

Abstract: The Large Vector Multiple (LVM) is the relevant gauge multiplet for gauging isometries acting on both the chiral and the twisted chiral fields in a $(2, 2)$ sigma model. Here we show that a recently proposed new gauge multiplet is a constrained or partially dualized version of the LVM. Gauging using this multiplet results in a $(2, 2)$ $βγ$ system interacting with a sigma model.

</details>


### [123] [The Classical Gravitational Impulse at High Energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18544)
*Michael Saavedra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在超相对论极限下，计算了两个经典有质量标量粒子在固定$G_N s/m b$下、至$O(m^4/s^2)$阶的引力冲量，并通过小质量区域的4点和5点散射振幅，重求和了所有大的$G_N s$修正，结果与后闵可夫斯基展开及无质量极限下的辐射能量结果完全一致，并预测了PM展开到11阶的领先高能行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超相对论极限下，经典引力散射中存在大量$G_N s$的修正项，这些项在标准后闵可夫斯基（PM）展开中难以直接处理。本文旨在通过振幅方法，系统地重求和这些大$G_N s$修正，从而获得更精确的经典引力冲量，并预测更高阶PM展开的高能行为。

Method: 在超相对论极限下，计算4点和5点散射振幅，考虑小质量区域（$-t\sim m^2$），并利用KMOC公式计算冲量，最后取大质量极限恢复经典结果。通过重求和所有大的$G_N s$修正，得到解析结果。

Result: 重求和后的结果与已知的后闵可夫斯基展开完全一致；在无质量极限下，恢复了前人对辐射能量的计算结果。利用重求和振幅，预测了PM展开到第11阶的领先高能行为。

Conclusion: 本文的方法有效重排了超相对论极限下的引力修正，为经典引力散射的高能行为提供了更完整的描述，并为更高阶PM展开提供了预测。

Abstract: We compute the gravitational impulse for two classical massive scalars in the ultrarelativistic limit to all orders in Newton's constant $G_N$ at fixed $G_N s/m b$ to $O(m^4/s^2)$. By computing the 4 and 5-point scattering amplitudes in the small-mass regime of $-t\sim m^2$, we are able to resum all large $G_N s$ corrections. Applying the KMOC formula for the impulse and taking the large mass limit, we recover the classical result at high energies. This resummation is in complete agreement with known results in the post-Minkowski expansion, and in the massless limit we recover previous results for the radiated energy. We use this resummed amplitude to predict the leading high-energy behavior of the PM expansion to eleventh post-Minkowski order.

</details>


### [124] [Modular Lower Bounds on Reeh-Schlieder State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18640)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Reeh-Schlieder定理表明任何目标态都可以由任意小区域中的局域算符从真空中近似得到，但未给出定量代价。本文从Tomita-Takesaki理论中提取一个模型无关的准备界：具有深度负模能量的目标需要大算符。缩放到物理收缩后，该界变为后选择开销的下界。在已知模哈密顿量的几何中，该界可显式化——Bisognano-Wichmann给出楔形区域的升压能量表述，Casini-Huerta-Myers公式给出共形场论有界区域的应力张量版本。局域幺正只能到达非负模能量态，负模扇区需要非幺正或后选择操作，这一准备代价界补充了type III局域代数中的真空盗用现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Reeh-Schlieder定理的定性结论（任意态可局域逼近）补充定量的准备代价分析，填补该定理缺乏资源开销估计的空白。

Method: 利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论的标准估计，推导出模能量与算符范数之间的不等式约束；通过缩放到物理收缩算符，将准备界转化为后选择开销下界；在具体几何（楔形区域、CFT有界区域）中利用已知的模哈密顿量显式化。

Result: 得到模型无关的准备代价下界：负模能量态的制备需要大范数局域算符，后选择开销随负模能量指数增长；局域幺正只能到达非负模能量态；该界与type III代数的真空盗用现象互补。

Conclusion: Reeh-Schlieder定理的操作代价可通过模理论定量刻画，负模能量态的制备必然伴随指数级的后选择开销，这一结果与量子场论中局域代数的类型III性质自洽。

Abstract: The Reeh-Schlieder theorem says that every target vector can be approximated from the vacuum by an operator localized in an arbitrarily small spacetime region, but it gives no quantitative cost for doing so. This note isolates a standard Tomita-Takesaki estimate as a model-independent preparation bound. Targets with deeply negative modular energy require large local operators. After rescaling such an operator to a physical contraction, the same estimate becomes a lower bound on postselection overhead. In geometries where the modular Hamiltonian is known, the bound becomes explicit. Bisognano-Wichmann turns it into a boost energy statement for wedges, and the Casini-Huerta-Myers formula gives a stress-tensor version for bounded regions of conformal field theories. Local unitaries can only reach states of nonnegative modular energy. Negative modular sectors require nonunitary or postselected outcomes, giving a preparation cost bound that complements vacuum embezzlement in type III local algebras.

</details>


### [125] [Signatures of Quantum Chaos in the D1D5 System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18725)
*Haoyu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究D1D5 CFT中随机矩阵统计的出现，通过分析低能近BPS扇区中的二阶提升矩阵。在平面大N极限下，单圈和多圈态之间的混合被抑制，能谱呈现泊松统计；而在有限N下，非平面项恢复这种混合，导致与随机矩阵行为一致的能级排斥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究D1D5 CFT中随机矩阵统计行为如何出现，理解在低能近BPS扇区中非平面循环结构混合与能级排斥之间的关系。

Method: 研究二阶提升矩阵，比较N=3有限N提升问题与固定orbifold共形权重和R荷下的平面大N极限。

Result: 平面大N极限下对称性分辨的提升谱呈现泊松统计；有限N下非平面项恢复循环结构混合，伴随与随机矩阵行为一致的能级排斥。

Conclusion: 在低能近BPS扇区中，有限N下的非平面循环结构混合与能级排斥及随机矩阵类间距统计的出现相关。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of random-matrix statistics in the D1D5 CFT by studying second-order lifting matrices in low-energy near-BPS sectors. We compare the $N=3$ finite-$N$ lifting problems with the planar large-$N$ limit at fixed orbifold conformal weight and R charges. In the planar large-$N$ limit at fixed orbifold energy, mixing between single-cycle and multi-cycle states is suppressed, and the symmetry-resolved lifting spectra display Poisson-like level statistics. At finite $N$, non-planar terms restore this mixing between different cycle structures. Within the resulting symmetry-resolved sectors, this finite-$N$ mixing is accompanied by level repulsion consistent with random-matrix behavior. These results suggest that, in the low-energy near-BPS sectors accessible to our analysis, non-planar cycle-structure mixing at finite $N$ is associated with the onset of level repulsion and random-matrix-like spacing statistics.

</details>

### [126] [At Most Two Infinite Blue Clusters in the CMR Representation of the Edwards-Anderson Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17338)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文证明了在短程Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃模型中，CMR表示的蓝色子图至多包含两个无限连通分支；若存在两个，则它们属于同一无限灰色簇且具有相反的重叠奇偶类。该结果通过联合测度、有限盒合并操作和质量输运界证明，克服了蓝色键过程既非插入容忍也非正关联的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃有序相的几何特征是开放问题，双副本CMR表示被提出作为自旋玻璃有序的几何标志。均场理论和数值结果表明低温相应存在两个携带相反重叠符号的宏观蓝色簇，但缺乏严格证明。本文旨在建立严格的约束条件，验证这一两簇图景的兼容性。

Method: 在平移不变的联合Gibbs测度下（包含无序、两个自旋副本和CMR键变量），通过有限盒合并操作和质量输运边界定理，证明蓝色子图至多有两个无限连通分支。利用贝叶斯重采样建立有限能量和渗流转变，通过基于奇偶性的Peierls估计分析灰色子图。克服蓝色键过程缺乏插入容忍性和正关联性的难点，通过联合测度框架替代传统Burton-Keane和随机簇参数。

Result: 证明了任何平移不变联合Gibbs测度下：(1) 蓝色子图至多包含两个无限连通分支；(2) 若存在两个无限蓝色分支，则它们位于同一无限灰色簇中且属于相反的重叠奇偶类。同时建立了灰色子图的有限能量性质和渗流转变。

Conclusion: 该结果并未证明无限蓝色簇的存在性或自旋玻璃相变，但为短程自旋玻璃的两簇图景提供了严格的上界约束，与均场理论和数值结果一致，是理解自旋玻璃有序相几何结构的重要理论进展。

Abstract: The two-replica Chayes-Machta-Redner (CMR) representation is one of the main proposed geometric signatures of spin-glass order in the short-range Edwards-Anderson model. Mean-field arguments and recent numerics suggest that the low-temperature phase should exhibit two macroscopic blue clusters carrying opposite overlap signs. We prove a rigorous structural constraint in this direction. For any translation-invariant joint Gibbs measure on disorder, two spin replicas, and CMR bond variables on Z^d, the blue subgraph contains at most two infinite connected components; if two exist, then they lie in a common infinite grey cluster and belong to opposite overlap-parity classes. The main obstacle is that the blue-bond process is neither insertion-tolerant nor positively associated, so the usual Burton-Keane and random-cluster arguments do not apply. We circumvent this by working in the full joint measure and using a finite-box merge operation together with the mass-transport bound on ends of translation-invariant subgraphs. As auxiliary input, we establish finite energy and a percolation transition for the grey subgraph via Bayesian resampling of couplings and a parity-based Peierls estimate. These results do not prove the existence of infinite blue clusters or a spin-glass phase transition, but they give a rigorous upper bound compatible with the two-cluster picture for short-range spin glasses.

</details>


### [127] [Anderson Transition and Mobility Edges in a Family of 3D Fractal Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17953)
*Tianyu Li,Xin Tang,Sheng Liu,Haiping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类谱维度在2到3之间连续可调的三维分形晶格，系统研究了非整数维度下的安德森相变。临界无序强度随谱维度从2到3从0连续变化至16.6，临界指数与谱维度呈近似反比关系，表明谱维度主导了相变普适类，而微观几何细节则影响临界点位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安德森局域化从根本上受维度控制，但连续可调的非整数维度下的安德森相变性质尚未被充分探索。

Method: 引入一类谱维度$d_s\in[2,3]$连续可调的三维分形晶格家族；采用大规模有限尺寸标度分析系统研究安德森相变并识别迁移率边。

Result: 临界无序强度从$0$（$d_s=2$）连续变化至$16.6$（$d_s=3$）；临界指数与$d_s$呈近似反比关系；谱维度主导相变普适类，微观几何细节影响临界点。

Conclusion: 可调分形晶格为探索超越常规整数维系统的局域化和量子临界现象提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Anderson localization is fundamentally controlled by dimensionality, yet the nature of the Anderson transition in continuously tunable noninteger dimensions remains largely unexplored. Here, we introduce a family of three-dimensional fractal lattices with continuously tunable spectral dimension $d_s\in[2,3]$, providing a controlled platform for studying localization physics beyond integer dimensions and across the lower critical dimension $d_s=2$. Using large-scale finite-size scaling analysis, we systematically investigate the Anderson transition and identify mobility edges throughout the fractal family. The critical disorder strength evolves continuously from $0$ to $16.6$ as the spectral dimension increases from $2$ to $3$. We show that the spectral dimension predominantly governs the universality class of the transition, while the precise critical point is additionally influenced by microscopic geometric details of the underlying fractal lattice. The critical exponent exhibits an approximate inverse dependence on $d_s$, providing quantitative insight into scaling theory in noninteger dimensions. Our results establish tunable fractal lattices as a versatile framework for exploring localization and quantum critical phenomena beyond conventional integer-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [128] [Localization Transitions in a Half-Filled Helical Aubry-André Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18064)
*Taylan Yildiz,B. Tanatar,Balázs Hetényi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展Aubry-André模型引入次近邻跳跃项，研究一维准周期晶格中的局域化转变。利用现代极化理论构造几何Binder累积量来提取临界势，发现长程跳跃稳定扩展相，并采用Zeckendorf-shift构造实现热力学极限下的可控分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Aubry-André模型仅考虑最近邻跳跃，不足以描述长程跳跃对局域化转变的影响。本文引入第N近邻跳跃项，扩展了准周期晶格局域化问题的控制参数空间，旨在揭示长程跳跃和准周期势竞争下的相变行为。

Method: 在非相互作用费米子体系中，利用现代极化理论计算多体Slater行列式基态的极化振幅，构造几何Binder累积量；通过其零交叉点提取临界势；采用Zeckendorf-shift构造沿Fibonacci序列系统尺寸实现可控热力学极限。

Result: 强螺旋跳跃（长程跳跃）将临界势向上推移，稳定扩展相；临界势随N变化呈现明显的公度性诱导尖峰；几何Binder累积量与费米能隙在扩展相区域表现一致。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃显著影响准周期晶格的局域化相变，几何Binder累积量是诊断该转变的有效工具，Zeckendorf-shift构造为斐波那契序列尺寸下的热力学极限提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: We study localization in a one-dimensional quasiperiodic lattice obtained by extending the Aubry-André model with an additional $N$th-neighbor hopping term of strength $J_{N}$. This long-range tunneling couples successive windings of an effective helical chain and introduces a second control parameter beyond the quasiperiodic potential strength $Δ$. Working with noninteracting fermions (typically at half filling), we diagnose the delocalization-localization transition using extensions of the modern theory of polarization. Specifically, we compute the polarization amplitudes of the many-body Slater-determinant ground state and construct a geometric Binder cumulant from polarization amplitudes. The critical potential where the localization transition happens is extracted from the sign change (zero crossing) of the geometric Binder cumulant. We map critical potential as a function of $J_N$ and the helical range $N$, finding that stronger helical hopping generally stabilizes the extended phase (shifting critical potential upward), while the $N$-dependence can display pronounced commensurability-induced spikes. We further compare the geometric Binder cumulant with the Fermi gap, which remains near zero at small values of potential and opens in the same parameter regime where the geometric Binder cumulant departs from extended phase. Finally, to take a controlled thermodynamic limit along Fibonacci system sizes, we employ a Zeckendorf-shift construction that fixes the many-body sector consistently as system size goes to infinity.

</details>

### [129] [Relativistic theory for coupled orbital and spin angular momentum dynamics in magnetic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16830)
*Subhadip Santra,Ritwik Mondal,Marco Berritta,Peter M. Oppeneer*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套完整的相对论理论框架，用以描述磁性系统中电子自旋角动量(S)和轨道角动量(L)的动力学行为。从Dirac-Kohn-Sham哈密顿量出发，通过幺正变换得到扩展的Pauli哈密顿量，推导了轨道和自旋角动量的耦合动力学方程。研究表明：总角动量J=S+L在无自旋极化交换场时守恒，但在电磁场（如激光脉冲、太赫兹场）作用下不再守恒；然而在原子尺度Heisenberg近似下，即使存在电磁场，总角动量仍然守恒，但自旋和轨道角动量各自不守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对磁性系统中电子角动量动力学的描述缺乏完整的相对论理论框架，尤其是自旋和轨道角动量之间的耦合动力学尚未在相对论Dirac框架下得到统一表述。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，建立自洽的相对论性角动量动力学理论。

Method: 从相对论Dirac-Kohn-Sham哈密顿量出发，在电磁场作用下应用幺正变换得到扩展的Pauli哈密顿量；基于半相对论哈密顿量推导轨道和自旋角动量的动力学方程；考虑总角动量J=S+L的守恒性；在原子尺度下引入Heisenberg交换相互作用近似，推导耦合的运动方程。

Result: (1) 在无自旋极化Kohn-Sham交换场时，总角动量J守恒，但在电磁场作用下不再守恒；(2) 在原子Heisenberg近似下，即使存在电磁场，总角动量仍然守恒，但原子自旋和轨道角动量各自不守恒。

Conclusion: 建立了相对论框架下自旋和轨道角动量耦合动力学的完整理论，明确了不同近似条件下总角动量、自旋角动量和轨道角动量的守恒特性，为理解磁性系统中超快自旋动力学和轨道动力学提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a complete relativistic theory to describe the dynamics of electronic angular momentum including both spin (S) and orbital (L) contributions in magnetic systems. We start with the relativistic Dirac-Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian under the influence of an electromagnetic field and apply a unitary transformation to formulate the extended Pauli Hamiltonian. Using the transformed semirelativistic Hamiltonian, we derive the angular momentum dynamics for the orbital and spin angular momenta. Thereby, we formulate the coupled dynamics of orbital and spin moments consistent with the relativistic Dirac framework. Considering especially the conservation of the total angular momentum, J = S +L, we show first that J is conserved in the absence of a spin-polarized Kohn-Sham exchange field, but is no longer conserved under the application of an electromagnetic field, e.g., laser pulse, THz field, etc. Second, considering magnetic systems with atomic spin and orbital momenta, we derive the coupled equations of motion of angular momenta dynamics whilst making the atomistic Heisenberg approximation for the exchange interaction. Our results suggest that, under these assumptions, the total angular momentum remains conserved, even with electromagnetic field, but atomic spin and orbital angular momenta individually are not conserved.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamic Many-Body Theory: The dynamics of atomic impurities in $^4$He](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17069)
*Eckhard Krotscheck*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过微观多体方法研究了$^4$He量子流体中原子杂质的动力学，包括μ子$^4$He、反质子$^4$He和μ子素原子，计算了化学势、有效质量及色散关系，发现μ子素杂质由于大零点运动探测短程原子相互作用，其化学势约19 meV，而反质子$^4$He具有负化学势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受μ子素在$^4$He中实验的启发，旨在检验不稳定粒子中性束缚态的引力自由落体普适性。

Method: 采用半解析方法进行结构计算，并将所有关联函数时间化处理来推导动力学，类比背景液体的动力学推导杂质自能的工整公式。

Result: μ子素杂质化学势约19 meV；反质子$^4$He具有负化学势；$^3$He和氢原子有效质量与实验吻合；μ子$^4$He和反质子$^4$He的色散关系穿过旋子极小值下方。

Conclusion: 该方法能够可靠描述量子流体中原子杂质的微观动力学行为，且杂质种类显著影响其化学势和色散关系。

Abstract: We implement manifestly microscopic many-body methods to study the dynamics of atomic impurities in a host quantum fluid, specifically $^4$He. Our investigations are motivated by experiments of muonium atoms within $^4$He with the goal of testing the universality of free fall by neutral bound states using unstable particles.
  Structure calculations are performed using standard semi-analytic methods; we extend here the calculations of our previos work (Journal of Low Temperature Physics {\bf 93}, 415-449 (1993)) to muonic \he4, antiprotonic \he4 and mounium atoms within \he4. We find that the muonium impurity probes, due to its large zero-point motion, the atomic interaction at rather short distances. Its chemical potential is estimated to be about 19 meV. Antiprotonic \he4 has, on the other hand, a negative chemical potential.
  Dynamics is treated by making all correlation functions time-dependent. In analogy to the derivation of the dynamics of the background liquid, we derive a working formula for the impurity self-energy that includes the most relevant physical effects. Results for the effective mass of \he3 and hydrogen atoms agree well with available experiments. The dispersion relations of muonic \he4 and antiprotonic \he4 pass through under the roton minimum.

</details>


### [131] [Coherent spectroscopy of collective excitations in superfluid helium far from equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17835)
*Gabriel Voith,Alexander A. Milner,Valery Milner*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文利用飞秒脉冲序列的超快相干控制技术，对超流氦中远离平衡态的Landau激发谱多个分支进行了光谱学研究，通过测量时间分辨光学双折射，追踪了maxon对和Pitaevskii plateau激发的非平衡动力学，揭示了maxon对惊人的强结合能、极短寿命以及准粒子有效质量对相干响应相位的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流体中集体激发的超快动力学在roton区域之外仍很大程度上未被探索，尽管这对理解这些系统中的非平衡过程至关重要。

Method: 采用飞秒脉冲序列的超快相干控制技术，通过测量时间分辨光学双折射来追踪非平衡动力学。

Result: 成功观测到maxon对和Pitaevskii plateau激发，发现maxon对具有异常强的结合能、极短的寿命，并揭示了准粒子有效质量对相干响应相位的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过探测飞秒到皮秒时间尺度的非平衡动力学，能够提取强相互作用量子流体中集体激发的先前无法获取的信息。

Abstract: Ultrafast dynamics of collective excitations in superfluids remains largely unexplored beyond the roton regime, despite its importance for understanding nonequilibrium processes in these systems. Here, we employ ultrafast coherent control with sequences of femtosecond pulses to perform spectroscopy of multiple branches of the Landau excitation spectrum in superfluid helium far from equilibrium. By measuring the time-resolved optical birefringence, we track the nonequilibrium dynamics of maxon pairs and Pitaevskii plateau excitations alongside the previously studied roton pairs, revealing surprisingly strong binding energy of maxon pairs, their extremely short lifetime, and the influence of the quasiparticle effective mass on the phase of the coherent response. These results demonstrate the ability to extract previously inaccessible information about collective excitations in a strongly interacting quantum fluid by probing its nonequilibrium dynamics on femtosecond and picosecond timescales.

</details>
